product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_target_qty_normalized': 0.0, 'close_confirmed': True, 'core_put_driver': 'MOM120负动量下限', 'core_put_target_delta': 0.25, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

_ test_ordinary_open_rejects_missing_source_or_duplicate_identity[missing-IC] _

product = 'IC', case = 'missing'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('product', ['IC', 'IM'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('case', ['missing', 'null', 'empty', 'whitespace', 'duplicate'])
    def test_ordinary_open_rejects_missing_source_or_duplicate_identity(product, case):
        signal = _ordinary_open_signal(product)
        prices = signal['v14_ordinary_put_open_prices']
        if case == 'missing':
            prices[0].pop('source')
        elif case == 'duplicate':
            prices.append({**prices[0], 'openprice': 99.0})
        else:
            prices[0]['source'] = {'null': None, 'empty': '', 'whitespace': '  '}[case]
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='来源|报价重复'):
>           state.validate_v14_new_signal(signal, product)

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_target_qty_normalized': 0.0, 'close_confirmed': True, 'core_put_driver': 'MOM120负动量下限', 'core_put_target_delta': 0.25, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('product', ['IC', 'IM'])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize('case', ['missing', 'null', 'empty', 'whitespace', 'duplicate'])
    def test_ordinary_open_rejects_missing_source_or_duplicate_identity(product, case):
        signal = _ordinary_open_signal(product)
        prices = signal['v14_ordinary_put_open_prices']
        if case == 'missing':
            prices[0].pop('source')

    def test_ic_reset_uses_existing_chain_failover(monkeypatch):
        """A valid backup exists, so an SSE outage must not abort monthly selection."""

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/Administrator.DESKTOP-95I7VVU/AppData/Local/Temp/icim_put_b40d1a0b/pytest/test_nonfuture_momentum_plan_s0')
product = 'IC', route = 'short_put', missing = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product,route", [
        ("IC", "short_put"), ("IC", "assigned_etf"), ("IC", "recovery_future"),
        ("IM", "short_put"), ("IM", "cash_wait"), ("IM", "recovery_future")])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", [False, True])
    def test_nonfuture_momentum_plan_survives_restart_and_requires_exact_open(tmp_path, product, route, missing):
        record = _record()
        _route(record["products"][product], product, route)
        store, record = _persist(tmp_path, record)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        signal = _signal(product, current, momentum=True)
        plan = signal["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["new_qty"] == 0.0
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False
        record = _append(store, record, product, signal)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        field = "verified_momentum_put_qty" if product == "IC" else "verified_momentum_put_qty_normalized"
        assert current[field] == 0.0
        next_day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))
        opening = _opening(product, current, next_day, missing=missing)
        assert opening["status"] == ("closed_missing_open_price" if missing else "confirmed_open_research_price")
        # Test the second append before fix10's new Fear requirements by retaining
        # the real next session Oct 8 and adding the shared explicit evidence below.
        signal = _signal(product, current, next_day, momentum=True, opening=opening)
        other = "IM" if product == "IC" else "IC"
        pair = {product: signal, other: _signal(other, state.anchors_from_record(record)[other], next_day)}
        for item in pair.values():
            item.update(score=3.0, grid_action="HOLD", grid_entry_source_current="none", grid_entry_source_target="none",
                        grid_transition_reason="hold_flat", fear_greed_index=40.0, fear_data_date=next_day,
                        fear_data_status="same_day_post_close", fear_source_url=bot.fear_grid.CSV_URL,
                        fear_csv_sha256="a" * 64, fear_page_updated_at=f"{next_day}T15:10:00+08:00",
                        fear_retrieved_at=f"{next_day}T18:00:00+08:00")
>       record = store.append_confirmed_signals(record, pair)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

poe_ic_im_mainline_v1_4_bot.py:4863: AttributeError
_ test_nonfuture_momentum_plan_survives_restart_and_requires_exact_open[False-IC-recovery_future] _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/Administrator.DESKTOP-95I7VVU/AppData/Local/Temp/icim_put_b40d1a0b/pytest/test_nonfuture_momentum_plan_s2')
product = 'IC', route = 'recovery_future', missing = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product,route", [
        ("IC", "short_put"), ("IC", "assigned_etf"), ("IC", "recovery_future"),
        ("IM", "short_put"), ("IM", "cash_wait"), ("IM", "recovery_future")])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", [False, True])
    def test_nonfuture_momentum_plan_survives_restart_and_requires_exact_open(tmp_path, product, route, missing):
        record = _record()
        _route(record["products"][product], product, route)
        store, record = _persist(tmp_path, record)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        signal = _signal(product, current, momentum=True)
        plan = signal["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["new_qty"] == 0.0
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False
        record = _append(store, record, product, signal)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        field = "verified_momentum_put_qty" if product == "IC" else "verified_momentum_put_qty_normalized"
        assert current[field] == 0.0
        next_day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))
        opening = _opening(product, current, next_day, missing=missing)
        assert opening["status"] == ("closed_missing_open_price" if missing else "confirmed_open_research_price")
        # Test the second append before fix10's new Fear requirements by retaining
        # the real next session Oct 8 and adding the shared explicit evidence below.
        signal = _signal(product, current, next_day, momentum=True, opening=opening)
        other = "IM" if product == "IC" else "IC"
        pair = {product: signal, other: _signal(other, state.anchors_from_record(record)[other], next_day)}
        for item in pair.values():
            item.update(score=3.0, grid_action="HOLD", grid_entry_source_current="none", grid_entry_source_target="none",
                        grid_transition_reason="hold_flat", fear_greed_index=40.0, fear_data_date=next_day,
                        fear_data_status="same_day_post_close", fear_source_url=bot.fear_grid.CSV_URL,
                        fear_csv_sha256="a" * 64, fear_page_updated_at=f"{next_day}T15:10:00+08:00",
                        fear_retrieved_at=f"{next_day}T18:00:00+08:00")
>       record = store.append_confirmed_signals(record, pair)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

product = 'IM'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target': '目标空仓（fix6停止卖Call）', 'call_target_contract': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

        record = _record()

        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]

        plan = signal["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]

        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False

        assert current[field] == 0.0
        next_day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))
        opening = _opening(product, current, next_day, missing=missing)
        assert opening["status"] == ("closed_missing_open_price" if missing else "confirmed_open_research_price")
        # Test the second append before fix10's new Fear requirements by retaining
        # the real next session Oct 8 and adding the shared explicit evidence below.
        signal = _signal(product, current, next_day, momentum=True, opening=opening)
        other = "IM" if product == "IC" else "IC"

        record = _record()

        store, record = _persist(tmp_path, record)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]

        plan = signal["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["new_qty"] == 0.0
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False
        record = _append(store, record, product, signal)
        current = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]

        assert current[field] == 0.0
        next_day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))
        opening = _opening(product, current, next_day, missing=missing)
        assert opening["status"] == ("closed_missing_open_price" if missing else "confirmed_open_research_price")
        # Test the second append before fix10's new Fear requirements by retaining
        # the real next session Oct 8 and adding the shared explicit evidence below.
        signal = _signal(product, current, next_day, momentum=True, opening=opening)

product = 'IM'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target': '目标空仓（fix6停止卖Call）', 'call_target_contract': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

product = 'IM'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target': '目标空仓（fix6停止卖Call）', 'call_target_contract': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product", ["IC", "IM"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", [None, "old_core", "new_core", "momentum"])
    def test_profit_and_momentum_execute_independently_after_restart(tmp_path, product, missing):

        anchor = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]

        assert initial["v14_action"] == "CORE_PUT_PROFIT3X_REENTER"

        assert ordinary["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False

        field = "verified_momentum_put_qty" if product == "IC" else "verified_momentum_put_qty_normalized"
        assert anchor[field] == 0
        assert anchor["v14_profit_pending"] is True
        day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):

poe_ic_im_mainline_v1_4_bot.py:4863: AttributeError
_ test_profit_execution_day_new_momentum_plan_preserves_new_core_cost[False-IC] _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/Administrator.DESKTOP-95I7VVU/AppData/Local/Temp/icim_put_b40d1a0b/pytest/test_profit_execution_day_new_0')
product = 'IC', trigger_momentum = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product", ["IC", "IM"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("trigger_momentum", [False, True])
    def test_profit_execution_day_new_momentum_plan_preserves_new_core_cost(tmp_path, product, trigger_momentum):
        store, record = _persist(tmp_path, _profit_record(product))
        initial = _profit_trigger(product, state.anchors_from_record(record)[product], momentum=trigger_momentum)
        record = _profit_append(store, record, product, initial)
        day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(DAY + timedelta(days=1))
        signal = _profit_session(product, state.anchors_from_record(record)[product], day, momentum=not trigger_momentum)
        assert signal["v14_action"] == "EXECUTE_CORE_PUT_PROFIT3X_REENTER"
        plan = signal["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["changed"] is False
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["old_contract"] == signal["v14_profit_open_executed_contract"]
        assert plan["legs"]["core"]["new_contract"] == signal["v14_profit_open_executed_contract"]
        record = _profit_append(store, record, product, signal)
        anchor = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        assert anchor["v14_core_put_entry_premium"] == (.35 if product == "IC" else 350.0)
        field = "verified_momentum_put_qty" if product == "IC" else "verified_momentum_put_qty_normalized"
        qty = 3 if product == "IC" else 1.5
        assert anchor[field] == (qty if trigger_momentum else 0)
        next_day = bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(day + timedelta(days=1))
        final = _profit_session(product, anchor, next_day, momentum=not trigger_momentum)
>       result = _profit_append(store, record, product, final)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:

            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")

monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x000002202B7BFAF0>
product = 'IC'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('product', ['IC','IM'])
    def test_short_entry_closes_future_held_after_same_day_quarter_roll(monkeypatch,product):
        monkeypatch.setattr(bot.fear_grid,'EFFECTIVE_SIGNAL_DATE',date(2100,1,1))
        # The daily close's quarterly roll completes before next session's short entry.
        day=date(2026,12,15 if product=='IC' else 17)
        previous=day-timedelta(days=1)
        record=fixture._record()
        record.update(verified_day=previous.isoformat())
        for a in record['products'].values():a['last_verified_day']=previous.isoformat()
        if product=='IM':
            record['products']['IC']['post_core_contract']='IC2703'
            original_roll=bot.quarter_roll.roll_state
            def with_next_listed(p,held,listed,*args):
                # Fixture hardcodes only Dec/Mar; real post-roll IC needs next June listed.
                if p=='IC' and held=='IC2703':listed=[*listed,'IC2706']
                return original_roll(p,held,listed,*args)
            monkeypatch.setattr(bot.quarter_roll,'roll_state',with_next_listed)
        record['digest']=state._digest(record)
        before=state.anchors_from_record(record);a=before[product]
        next_day=bot._roll_forward_exchange_day(day+timedelta(days=1))
        quarter=bot.quarter_roll.roll_state(product,product+'2612',[product+'2612',product+'2703'],
                     day,True,lambda c:bot._third_friday(*bot._contract_month(c)),bot._is_exchange_trading_day)
        assert quarter['roll_confirmed']
        replacement=candidate(product)
        replacement.update(contract='510500P2701M06000' if product=='IC' else 'MO2701-P-6200',
                           expiry=date(2027,1,27 if product=='IC' else 15))
        other='IM' if product=='IC' else 'IC'
        signals={product:fixture._signal(product,a,day,candidate=replacement,
                        extra=dict(momentum_120=.1,valuation_tier=1,valuation_puts_per_full_core=1,
                                   **({'put_reference_future':quarter['core_eod_contract']} if product=='IM' else {}))),
                 other:fixture._signal(other,before[other],day)}
        for s in signals.values():s['v14_opening_events']=[]
>       after=state.derive_next_anchors(record,signals)[product]
              ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _require_official_exchange_calendar(day: date, label: str) -> None:

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/Administrator.DESKTOP-95I7VVU/AppData/Local/Temp/icim_put_b40d1a0b/pytest/test_failed_evidence_preserves0')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x000002202B87D7F0>
product = 'IC'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product", ["IC", "IM"])
    def test_failed_evidence_preserves_hash_ledger_then_original_day_recovers(tmp_path, monkeypatch, product):
        import test_ic_im_ordinary_put_route_fix_20261013 as ledger_fixture
        # Exercise the new gate against an existing complete pre-Fear fixture;
        # dates and prices are explicitly synthetic, not historical return evidence.
        monkeypatch.setattr(execution, "OPEN_EVIDENCE_EFFECTIVE_DATE", ledger_fixture.DAY)
        record = ledger_fixture._record()
        decoded = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        previous = ledger_fixture._signal(product, decoded, ledger_fixture.PREVIOUS, momentum=True)
        record["products"][product]["v14_ordinary_put_pending"] = previous["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        store, record = ledger_fixture._persist(tmp_path, record)
        before = {p.relative_to(tmp_path).as_posix():p.read_bytes() for p in tmp_path.rglob("*.json")}
        anchor = state.anchors_from_record(record)[product]
        signal = ledger_fixture._signal(product, anchor, momentum=True)
        signal.update(v14_ordinary_put_open_status=RETRY,
                      v14_ordinary_put_open_plan=anchor["v14_ordinary_put_pending"])
        other = "IM" if product == "IC" else "IC"
        signals = {product: signal, other: ledger_fixture._signal(other, state.anchors_from_record(record)[other])}
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="unresolved opening evidence"):
            store.append_confirmed_signals(record, signals)
        assert {p.relative_to(tmp_path).as_posix():p.read_bytes() for p in tmp_path.rglob("*.json")} == before
        # Disguising the failure as the old closed status or omitting it must not
        # bypass the new-write check against the actual persisted plan.
        for status in ["closed_missing_open_price", None]:
            signals[product]["v14_ordinary_put_open_status"] = status
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="unresolved opening evidence"):
                store.append_confirmed_signals(record, signals)
        opening = ledger_fixture._opening(product, anchor, ledger_fixture.DAY)
        assert opening["status"] == "confirmed_open_research_price"
        signals[product] = ledger_fixture._signal(product, anchor, momentum=True, opening=opening)
>       updated = store.append_confirmed_signals(record, signals)
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

        monkeypatch.setattr(execution, "OPEN_EVIDENCE_EFFECTIVE_DATE", ledger.DAY)
        r = ledger._record()

        if damage == "missing": prices.pop()
        elif damage == "duplicate": prices.append(deepcopy(prices[0]))
        elif damage == "wrong_day": prices[0]["price_day"] = ledger.PREVIOUS
        elif damage == "empty_source": prices[0]["source"] = ""
        elif damage == "infinite": prices[0]["openprice"] = float("inf")
        else: prices[0]["contract"] = "FAKE-PUT"
        signals = {p: signal if p == product else ledger._signal(p, anchors[p]) for p in PRODUCTS}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product", PRODUCTS)

    def test_ordinary_forged_prices_fail_before_any_hash_ledger_write(tmp_path, monkeypatch, product, damage):
        monkeypatch.setattr(execution, "OPEN_EVIDENCE_EFFECTIVE_DATE", ledger.DAY)

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)

            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/Administrator.DESKTOP-95I7VVU/AppData/Local/Temp/icim_put_b40d1a0b/pytest/test_ordinary_forged_prices_fa6')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x000002202B5E88A0>
product = 'IC', damage = 'empty_source'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("product", PRODUCTS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["missing", "duplicate", "wrong_day", "empty_source", "infinite", "wrong_identity"])
    def test_ordinary_forged_prices_fail_before_any_hash_ledger_write(tmp_path, monkeypatch, product, damage):
        monkeypatch.setattr(execution, "OPEN_EVIDENCE_EFFECTIVE_DATE", ledger.DAY)
        r = ledger._record()
        previous = ledger._signal(product, state.anchors_from_record(r)[product], ledger.PREVIOUS, momentum=True)
        r["products"][product]["v14_ordinary_put_pending"] = previous["v14_ordinary_put_pending"]
        store, r = ledger._persist(tmp_path, r)
        anchors = state.anchors_from_record(r)
        opening = ledger._opening(product, anchors[product], ledger.DAY)
        signal = ledger._signal(product, anchors[product], momentum=True, opening=opening)
        prices = signal["v14_ordinary_put_open_prices"]
        if damage == "missing": prices.pop()
        elif damage == "duplicate": prices.append(deepcopy(prices[0]))
        elif damage == "wrong_day": prices[0]["price_day"] = ledger.PREVIOUS
        elif damage == "empty_source": prices[0]["source"] = ""
        elif damage == "infinite": prices[0]["openprice"] = float("inf")
        else: prices[0]["contract"] = "FAKE-PUT"
        signals = {p: signal if p == product else ledger._signal(p, anchors[p]) for p in PRODUCTS}
        before = {p.relative_to(tmp_path).as_posix(): p.read_bytes() for p in tmp_path.rglob("*.json")}
>       with pytest.raises(RuntimeError): store.append_confirmed_signals(r, signals)

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

product = 'IC'
signal = {'call_action': 'HOLD', 'call_current_contract': None, 'call_target_contract': None, 'call_target_expiry': None, ...}

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")
    
        def contract_covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if not value:
                return
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:IC|IM|MO)([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})(?:-[PC]-[0-9]+)?", str(value))
            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
    
        covered(signal.get("market_date"), "market date")
        covered(signal.get("next_trade_date"), "next session")
        # next_core and quote-frame expiries are diagnostics until actually adopted.
        contract_covered(signal.get("core_target"), "core target")
        for target, current in (("put_target_contract", "put_current_contract"),
                                ("v14_core_put_contract", "v14_core_put_current_contract"),
                                ("momentum_put_target_contract", "momentum_put_current_contract")):
            if signal.get(target) != signal.get(current):
                contract_covered(signal.get(target), target)
        if signal.get("v14_action") in {"ENTER_SHORT_PUT", "ROLL_SHORT_PUT"}:
            covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_expiry"), "new short Put expiry")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_short_put_contract"), "new short Put")
        if signal.get("v14_profit_pending"):
            covered(signal.get("v14_profit_execution_day"), "profit reentry execution")
            contract_covered(signal.get("v14_profit_reentry_contract"), "profit reentry")
        for key in ("v14_ordinary_put_open_plan", "v14_ordinary_put_open_pending", "v14_short_put_open_pending"):
            plan = signal.get(key)
            if not isinstance(plan, dict):
                continue
            covered(plan.get("execution_day"), key)
            contract_covered(plan.get("new_contract"), key)
            for leg in plan.get("legs", []):
>               if leg.get("changed") and float(leg.get("new_qty", 0) or 0) > 0:
                   ^^^^^^^
E               AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize('product', ['IC','IM'])

        # A consistent synthetic post-close wall clock exercises all unchanged
        # source/generation/write-time checks; no effective date is moved.

            monkeypatch.setattr(module,'datetime',SyntheticCloseClock)

        state._validate_record(record)

        store._atomic_write(store.latest_path,record)
        record=store.load_latest()

                   for side in ['old','new'] if float(leg[f'{side}_qty'])>0}

        frame.attrs.update(source='synthetic_dated_open',source_date=DAY)
        monkeypatch.setattr(bot,'fetch_cffex_quotes',lambda *args:frame)

    def require_signal_calendar(product: str, signal: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Guard consumed signal dates without treating every listed row as a target."""
        def covered(value: Any, label: str) -> None:
            if value is not None:
                day = value if isinstance(value, date) else date.fromisoformat(str(value)[:10])
                _require_official_exchange_calendar(day, f"{product} {label}")

            if match is None:
                match = re.fullmatch(r"510500P([0-9]{2})([0-9]{2})[MA][0-9]{5}", str(value))
            if match is not None:
                covered(date(2000 + int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2)), 1), label)
